# Sonic Pocket Adventure (Neo Geo Pocket Color, World) - TLCS-900/H program symbols
#
# Addresses and names only: no game data lives in this file.
# Format:  ADDRESS  KIND  NAME   [# comment]
#   KIND: func = function entry, code = code label, data = data label (the cartridge, 200000-3FFFFF);
#         io = a register of the TMP95C061 and the console's ports (00-FF), var = the game's work RAM
#         (4000-6BFF), bios = the BIOS's work RAM (6C00-6FFF), z80 = the Z80's RAM (7000-7FFF), k2ge = the
#         K2GE's registers and palettes, video = its sprite, map and tile memory, const = a value (the
#         disassembly's IDA segments: CPUREG, WORK_RAM, BIOS_RAM, Z80_RAM, VREGS, VRAM; const outside them)
# The names and comments are Valley Bell's disassembly's (IDA's names where it kept them), written from
# AS's listing by tools/listing_map.py.
